Batman Hood custom moveset armed 1.0 by ZeidCeZeY:

MAIN:
Set RPawnPlayerCombat CharacterName DLCRedHood

Set RAddContentPlayerCharacterMesh CapeMesh SkeletalMesh'DLC_Batman_Beyond_Mesh.Mesh.DLC_Batman_Beyond_Skin_Cape'

Set BmGame.RCombatMove_BatmanComboTakedown JokerHallucinationFovModifier 20

Set RPawnPlayer ForensicsType Class'BmScript.MRedHoodForensicsDevice'

Set RPawnPlayerCombat SecondaryGroupCombatMove Class'BmScript.RCombatMove_NightwingGroupStun'



IDLE:
Set MPawnPlayerConfig CombatIdlesAnimSet AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Combat.Code.RH_Combat_Idle_Overlays'
Set RPawnPlayerConfig DefaultIdleOverlayAnimSet AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Movement.Code.RH_Stand_Idle_Overlay'
Set RPawnPlayerConfig CoverIdleOverlayAnimSet AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Movement.Code.RH_Cover_Idle_Overlay'
Set RPawnPlayerConfig DangleIdleOverlayAnimSet AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Climb.Code.RH_Dangle_Idle_Overlay'
Set RPawnPlayerConfig HangIdleOverlayAnimSet AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Climb.Code.RH_Hang_Idle_Overlay'



MOVEMENT:
Set RPawnPlayerConfig MovementAnimSets (AnimSet'DLC0_Anim_RedHood_Movement.RH_Movement',AnimSet'Anim_Batman_Gadget.Code.BM_Radio',AnimSet'DLC_Anim_Nightwing_Movement.Code.NW_Movement',AnimSet'Anim_Batman_Movement.Code.BM_Movement',AnimSet'Anim_Joker.Code.Phase3_BM_Movement')


COMBAT SETS:
